Let's refine `project_feasible` to exactly sum. Approach:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0,1)
    target=0.5*n
    for _ in range(3):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
       if diff>0:
          mask=h<1
          cap= (1-h)*mask; tot=cap.sum()
          if tot==0: # all ones but target>n? infeasible; set some
             # distribute by setting? not happen if feasible
          h[mask]+=diff/tot*cap[mask]
       else:
          mask=h>0
          tot=h[mask].sum()
          h[mask]+=diff/tot*h[mask]
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       if diff>0:
          order=np.argsort(1-h)[::-1]? Need add to min cap? Actually choose element with most room. idx=int(np.argmax(1-h)); h[idx]=min(1,h[idx]+diff)
       else: idx=int(np.argmax(h)); h[idx]=max(0,h[idx]+diff)
    # if still not exact due edge (diff > cap), adjust multiple
    # But target feasible, after proportional should close.
    return h
```
This may not get sum within 1e-12 if one element not enough. We can loop adding to multiple if diff not fully absorbed:
```
while abs(diff)>1e-12:
   if diff>0:
      idx=int(np.argmax(1-h)); add=min(1-h[idx],diff); h[idx]+=add; diff-=add
   else:
      idx=int(np.argmax(h)); sub=min(h[idx],-diff); h[idx]-=sub; diff+=sub
```
Loop maybe O(n) worst but rare.

However, repeated project calls with final loop could be slow if many. Use one loop with at most n? okay.

But sum target can be .5 for odd n; we ensure n even. If initial odd, resample to even. All n in list even.

For other lags, c_new = c + a*(x-hp). `a` can be positive or negative. Feasibility for t:
- If a>0, require x ≥ (t-b)/a where b=c - a*hp? In previous code: b = cseg - a*hp. Since cseg + a*(x-hp) = b + a x. For a>0: b+a x ≥t -> x ≥ (t-b)/a. For a<0: x ≤ (t-b)/a (denom negative) -> previous formula `(mid - b_neg)/a_neg`, since both negative? mid-b negative? It computes max over (t-b)/a. Good.
- q: x(1-x) ≤t if t≥qconst? Wait c_center_new = qconst + x(1-x). For it ≤t: x(1-x) ≤ t - qconst = g. If g < -tol infeasible. If g ≥0.25 all. If 0≤g<0.25, x ≤ r1 or x ≥ r2. Previous is correct.
- constant unaff: require t≥unaff.
Binary search low/high. High should be current f at hp? It sets high max(unaff,qconst,qcur,cm)+1e-8. cm=max(cseg). Since at x=hp, affected values equal cseg (or qconst+qcur=c_center) and unaffected=unaff. f(hp)=max(unaff,cseg,c_center). high = max +1e-8. Good. low initialized max(0,unaff,qconst?) **Coordinate polish implementation** in detail:

```
def cd_polish(h,n,passes,deadline,project_every=10,stop_tol=1e-12,max_time=...):
    h=project_feasible(h,n.copy?)
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h); cur=float(c.max()); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cur*2/n
    noimprove=0
    for it in range(passes):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       improved_pass=False
       for p in range(n):
           if time.time()>deadline: break
           # extract lo hi etc using previous
           ...
           bx,bf=optimize_coordinate(...)
           if bx != h[p] and bf < cur - 1e-10? Use 1e-9 for time? If we only update when lowers cur enough, may miss tiny but overall. Use 1e-10.
              update
              improved_pass=True
           if (p+1)%project_every==0:
              h=project_feasible(h,n)
              c=np.correlate(...); cur=...
       h=project_feasible(...); c=...; cur=...
       cval=cur*2/n
       if cval < best_c - 1e-12:
          best_c=cval; best_h=h.copy(); noimprove=0
       else:
          noimprove+=1
          if noimprove>=3 and stop_tol...: break
    return best_h,best_c
```

```
def logsum_gradh(h,n,alpha,penlam):
 c=correlate(h,1-h); z=alpha*c; z-=z.max(); w=np.exp(z); w/=w.sum(); center=n-1; g=zeros(n)
 for m in range(2*n-1):
   wm=w[m]; if skip
   s=center-m
   if s>0:
      g[:n-s] += wm*(1-h[s:])
      g[s:] -= wm*h[:n-s]
   elif s<0:
      r=-s
      g[r:] += wm*(1-h[:n-r])
      g[:n-r] -= wm*h[r:]
   else: g += wm*(1-2*h)
 g *=2/n
 if penlam: g += 2*penlam*(h.sum()-n/2)*np.ones(n)
 return g
```

```
def optimize_coordinate(hp, cseg, a, qconst, unaff, cur, iters=18):
    # cseg excludes center, same length as a.

    else: cm=0.0

    if fcur >= cur + 1e-14: fcur=cur? Actually cur should equal fcur if c consistent. But due max maybe cur = fcur. Use fcur for high.
    b = cseg - a*hp  # length m

    zmax = b[zero].max() if zero.any() else -1e100
    low = max(0.0, unaff, qconst)  # q min qconst at endpoints if qconst>=0, else 0? Good.
    if zmax > low: low=zmax
    high = max(fcur, low) + 1e-8
    a_pos=a[pos]; a_neg=a[neg]; b_pos=b[pos]; b_neg=b[neg]
    pc=...; nc=...
    for _ in range(iters):
       mid=(low+high)/2
       feas = (mid >= unaff-tol) and (mid >= qconst-tol) and (mid >= zmax-tol)
       if feas:
          L=0; U=1
          if nc: L=max(L, max((mid-b_neg)/a_neg))  # a_neg negative

                disc=1-4g; r1=0.5-0.5sqrt; r2=0.5+0.5sqrt

       if feas: high=mid else low=mid

          else branches=[]

             if Lb<=Ub+tol: cands.extend([Lb,Ub,0.5*(Lb+Ub)])
    best_x=hp; best_f=fcur
    for x in cands:

          if f<best_f: best_f=f; best_x=x

    return hp,best_f? If no candidate improves but high lower? Could return hp to be safe.
```

```
def coord_eval(x,cseg,a,qconst,unaff):

 else v=0
```

```
def cd_polish(h,n,passes,deadline,project_every=10,stop_tol=1e-11,max_noimprove=4,min_passes=1):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full'); cur=float(c.max()); dx=2/n; best_c=cur*dx; best_h=h.copy()
    noim=0
    order = np.arange(n)
    for it in range(max(1,passes)):
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       improved=False
       # maybe randomize order after each pass? Pass param `randomize`: if rng is None? We don't pass rng to avoid closure. Could use global? Better create order within using a local counter? No closures okay. We can pass `seed`? Use `np.random.randint`? We can pass `r`? Simpler: deterministic natural order but alternate start offset? `order = np.roll(np.arange(n), start*7)` for pass. Or use `rng.permutation(n)` if we pass `rng`. We can pass `rng=None`; but `rng` is top-level? It can be passed.
       for idx in order:
          p=int(idx)
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          hp=h[p]
          center=n-1
          lo=min(p, center-p)
          hi=max(center+p, 2*center-p)
          seg=hi-lo+1
          s=center - np.arange(lo, hi+1, dtype=int)
          j=p+s; i=p-s
          a=np.zeros(seg,float)
          vj=(j>=0)&(j<n); vi=(i>=0)&(i<n)
          if np.any(vj): a[vj]+=1-h[j[vj]]
          if np.any(vi): a[vi]-=h[i[vi]]
          off=center-lo
          cseg=np.empty(seg-1,float); asg=np.empty(seg-1,float)
          if off>0:
             cseg[:off]=c[lo:center]; asg[:off]=a[:off]
          if hi>center:
             cseg[off:]=c[center+1:hi+1]; asg[off:]=a[off+1:]
          qconst=c[center]-hp*(1-hp)
          unaff=0.0
          if lo>0: unaff=max(unaff, float(c[:lo].max()))
          if hi+1 < len(c): unaff=max(unaff, float(c[hi+1:].max()))
          bx,bf=optimize_coordinate(hp,cseg,asg,qconst,unaff,cur,18)
          if bx != hp:
             # quick eval bf; if bf < cur - 1e-10
             dxv=bx-hp
             if off>0: c[lo:center]+=dxv*asg[:off]
             if hi>center: c[center+1:hi+1]+=dxv*asg[off:]
             c[center]=qconst+bx*(1-bx)
             h[p]=bx
             cur=max(cur,bf) # bf is new max? But c array updated; bf=coord_eval for new max. Could just bf. However if due numerical, cur=max(bf, maybe old unaffected?) bf includes unaff. Good.
             improved=True
          if (p+1)%project_every==0:
             h=project_feasible(h,n)
             c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full'); cur=float(c.max())
       h=project_feasible(h,n); c=...; cur=...
       cval=cur*dx
       if cval < best_c - 1e-12:
          best_c=cval; best_h=h.copy(); noim=0
       else:
          noim+=1
          if it>=min_passes and noim>=max_noimprove: break
       if improved and noim==0: order=order[1:]+order[:1]? or natural? Maybe random alternate.
    return best_h,best_c
```

Let's write `smooth_refine` compact:

```
def logsum_obj(x,n,alpha,pen):
    c=np.correlate(x,1-x,mode='full')
    z=alpha*c
    zm=float(z.max())
    val=((zm + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z-zm))))/alpha)*(2.0/n)
    if pen:
       s=float(x.sum())-0.5*n
       val += pen*s*s
    return val

def logsum_jac(x,n,alpha,pen):
    c=np.correlate(x,1-x,mode='full')
    z=alpha*c; z-=z.max(); w=np.exp(z); w/=w.sum()
    center=n-1; g=np.zeros(n)
    for m in range(2*n-1):
      wm=float(w[m])
      if wm <=1e-15: continue
      s=center-m
      if s>0:
        g[:n-s] += wm*(1-x[s:])
        g[s:] -= wm*x[:n-s]
      elif s<0:
        r=-s
        g[r:] += wm*(1-x[:n-r])
        g[:n-r] -= wm*x[r:]
      else:
        g += wm*(1-2*x)
    g *= 2.0/n
    if pen:
      g += 2*pen*(float(x.sum())-0.5*n)
    return g

def smooth_refine(h,n,deadline,alphas=(100,800),pen=500.0,maxiter=30):
   h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=compute_c(h,n); best=h
   bounds=[(0,1)]*n
   for alpha in alphas:
      if time.time()>deadline-30: break
      try:
        res=minimize(logsum_obj,best,args=(n,alpha,pen),method='L-BFGS-B',jac=logsum_jac,bounds=bounds,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
        x=project_feasible(res.x,n); c=compute_c(x,n)
        if c<best_c: best,best_c=x,c
      except Exception: pass
   return best,best_c
```
But `logsum_jac` uses `g += scalar` adds to all? If scalar is array? `2*pen*(sum-target)` is scalar; `g += scalar` works. Need ensure no in-place with integer? float. Good.

Bounds list size n up to 600; creation each call cost small. Pass bounds to smooth_refine to avoid recreate? Can define inside. No closures. `minimize` may call objective many times; `logsum_obj` with penalty uses c correlate O(n^2) C. For n=500, okay. But L-BFGS-B with penalty may wander out of feasible sum; project after.

However `logsum_obj` for alpha high, logsum large (could overflow if alpha*c > 700). c up to n, alpha 20000 => 1e7, exp(z-zm) where z-zm ≤0, no overflow; good.

Penalty 500 may dominate; gradient scaled. We can set pen=100. Sum drift during optimization might be large; penalty quadratic. Maybe use SLSQP equality? L-BFGS-B no constraint; if pen too low, final sum off but project. It may optimize infeasible region. Could be okay. We can call after CD when near feasible. Now, **make_start** function. We need use `resample_h` to change n. We can write:
```
def make_start(best,n,rng,typ):
    base=resample_h(best,n)
    if typ==0:
       x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2/n
       delta=0
       for k in range(1,9):
          delta += rng.normal(0,0.06/k)*np.cos(k*np.pi*(x-1))
          delta += rng.normal(0,0.04/k)*np.sin(k*np.pi*(x-1))
       return project_feasible(base+delta,n)
    if typ==1:
       return binary_top_h(base,n)
    if typ==2:
       out=np.zeros(n); idx=rng.choice(n,n//2,replace=False); out[idx]=1; return out
    if typ==3:
       return project_feasible(0.5*(base+base[::-1]),n)
    if typ==4:
       l=rng.randint(0,n); w=rng.randint(5,max(6,n//5)); hi=min(n,l+w)
       # add cosine bump random sign
       wgt=0.5+0.5*np.cos(np.pi*(np.arange(hi-l)/max(1,w-1)))
       h=base.copy(); h[l:hi]+=rng.normal(0,0.08)*wgt
       return project_feasible(h,n)
    if typ==5:
       # 5-level random
       return level_h(n,rng)
    return binary_top_h(base,n)
```
Need `level_h`:
```
def level_h(n,rng,levels=5):
    vals=np.linspace(0,1,levels)
    out=np.zeros(n)
    i=0
    while i<n:
       if i+levels<=n:
          out[i:i+levels]=vals[rng.permutation(levels)]
          i+=levels
       else:
          # fill remaining random vals
          rem=n-i; out[i:]=vals[rng.choice(rem+? no, size rem, replace=True)]
          i=n
    return project_feasible(out,n)
```
This uses random.choice with replace True; sum not exact but project. For remainder, choose random values not necessarily balance; project. Fine. For n large, loops n/5 (~200) okay. But `rng.permutation` 200 times maybe okay. Could do algorithmic tile then permutations? Fine.

```
def coord_vector_eval(xs,cseg,a,qconst,unaff):
   if a.size:
      v=np.max(cseg[None,:]+xs[:,None]*a[None,:],axis=1)
   else: v=np.zeros_like(xs)
   q=qconst+xs*(1-xs)
   return np.maximum(np.maximum(v,q),unaff)
```

```
def optimize_coordinate(hp,cseg,a,qconst,unaff,cur,iters=16):
  ... compute lower upper ...
  if low >= cur - 1e-11: return hp, cur (or fcur)
  bisect for feasibility(t)
  t=high
  # build feasible x list
  L=0; U=1; (respect t >= constants)
  compute line bounds
  q branches
  xlist=[Lb,Ub,mid] for branches; plus 0,1,hp
  evaluate vectorized; choose min f
  if best_f < cur - 1e-10: return best_x,best_f else hp, fcur
```

Feasibility check code:
```
for _ in range(iters):
 mid=(low+high)*0.5
 ok = mid >= unaff - tol and mid >= qconst - tol and (not zero or mid>=zmax - tol)
 if ok:
   L=0.; U=1.
   if nc:
      # for a_neg negative, upper bounds: (mid-b_neg)/a_neg
      # Use np.max; if any nan? no
      v=np.max((mid - b_neg)/a_neg)
      if v > L: L=v
   if pc:
      v=np.min((mid - b_pos)/a_pos)
      if v < U: U=v
   if L <= 1.0 + tol and U >= -tol:
```

One concern: The `optimize_coordinate` implementation with vectorized feasible interval may be more code but faster and exact. Let's write it carefully.

```
def optimize_coordinate(hp, cseg, a, qconst, unaff, cur, iters=16):
    m = a.size
    if m:
        cm = float(np.max(cseg))
    else:
        cm = 0.0
    qcur = qconst + hp*(1.0-hp)
    fcur = max(unaff, cm, qcur)
    if fcur <= cur:
        fcur = max(fcur, cur)
    # if current coordinate cannot lower
    b = cseg - a*hp if m else np.empty(0)
    pos = a > 1e-14 if m else np.zeros(0, dtype=bool)
    neg = a < -1e-14 if m else np.zeros(0, dtype=bool)
    zero = ~(pos|neg) if m else np.ones(0, dtype=bool)
    zmax = float(b[zero].max()) if zero.any() else -1e100
    low = max(0.0, unaff, qconst)
    if zmax > low: low = zmax
    if low >= fcur - 1e-11:
        return hp, fcur
    high = fcur + 1e-10
    a_pos = a[pos]; a_neg = a[neg]
    b_pos = b[pos]; b_neg = b[neg]
    pc = a_pos.size; nc = a_neg.size
    tol=1e-11
    for _ in range(iters):
       mid=0.5*(low+high)
       feas = (mid >= unaff - tol) and (mid >= qconst - tol) and (mid >= zmax - tol)
       if feas:
           L=0.0; U=1.0
           if nc:
               v=float(np.max((mid - b_neg)/a_neg)); 
               if v > L: L=v
           if pc:
               v=float(np.min((mid - b_pos)/a_pos))
               if v < U: U=v
           if L <= 1.0 + tol and U >= -tol:
               g=mid-qconst
               if g > 0.25:
                   feas=True
               elif g >= -tol:
                   disc=max(0.0,1.0-4.0*g)
                   r1=0.5-0.5*np.sqrt(disc); r2=0.5+0.5*np.sqrt(disc)
                   if (max(0.0,L) <= min(r1,U)+tol) or (max(r2,L) <= min(1.0,U)+tol):
                       feas=True
       if feas: high=mid
       else: low=mid
    t=high
    cand=[0.0,1.0,hp,0.5]
    if t >= unaff - tol and t >= qconst - tol and t >= zmax - tol:
       L=0.0; U=1.0
       if nc:
          v=float(np.max((t-b_neg)/a_neg)); L=max(L,v)
       if pc:
          v=float(np.min((t-b_pos)/a_pos)); U=min(U,v)
       if L <= 1.0+tol and U >= -tol:
          g=t-qconst
          branches=[]
          if g > 0.25:
             branches=[(0.0,1.0)]
          elif g >= -tol:
             disc=max(0.0,1-4*g); r1=...; r2=...; branches=[(0.0,r1),(r2,1.0)]
          for Lq,Uq in branches:
             Lb=max(0.0,L,Lq); Ub=min(1.0,U,Uq)
             if Lb <= Ub + tol:
                mid=(Lb+Ub)*0.5
                cand.extend([Lb,Ub,mid,Lb+0.25*(Ub-Lb),Lb+0.75*(Ub-Lb)])
    xs=np.array(cand)
    xs=np.clip(xs,0.0,1.0)
    vals=coord_evals(xs,cseg,a,qconst,unaff)
    ii=int(np.argmin(vals))
    bx=float(xs[ii]); bf=float(vals[ii])
    if bf < fcur - 1e-10:
       return bx,bf
    return hp,fcur
```
Need `coord_evals`:
```
def coord_evals(xs,cseg,a,qconst,unaff):
    xs=np.asarray(xs,float)
    if a.size:
       v=np.max(cseg[None,:]+xs[:,None]*a[None,:],axis=1)
    else: v=np.zeros(xs.shape)
    q=qconst + xs*(1-xs)
    return np.maximum(np.maximum(v,q), unaff)
```
If cseg empty but a empty, OK. If xs contains duplicates, fine.

Now, let's think if the coordinate descent can actually improve below 0.3808. There is risk. Could we add a **constraint-targeting heuristic**: If current C just above target, identify active lags where c_s near max. Then perform small coordinated changes using gradient projection. SLSQP/smooth_refine does this. Good.

Maybe use **active-constraint coordinate**: In cd_polish, if a coordinate's `unaff` (unaffected max) equals current max, then `low=unaff`, low>=cur, returns no update; coordinate cannot reduce max constraints. So only coordinates affecting active max constraints can improve. That's correct. Near optimum, many coordinates won't move. Global restarts needed.

Could add **active set restart**: Identify lags m with c>m threshold (max-epsilon). For each active lag, choose a coordinate pair adjacent in that lag and swap values? For example if lag s has large c, to reduce it, if h_i high (as first) and h_{i+s} high (as second? c term product? Actually for positive lag, c decreases if h_i low or h_{i+s} high. To reduce c_s, we can move mass from h_i to h_{i+s} (increase second) or vice versa for negative. We can perform **pair transfer** that directly reduces active lags. This can escape coordinate local. Let's consider implementing a simple pair move:
- For active lag s, find pairs (i,i+s) where h_i=1 high and h_{i+s}=0 low, transfer small mass: h_i -= δ, h_{i+s} += δ (preserve sum). For positive c_s, this reduces term h_i(1-h_{i+s}) if transfer to second? Yes if h_i decreases, h_{i+s} increases. It also affects other lags. For negative lag, transfer opposite. We can do random active pair sweeps with hill climbing. This is a different move not captured by single coordinate (because two coords). Could help.

Let's design `pair_move_optim(h,n,deadline,iters,rng)`:
- Keep c and cur.
- At each iteration, choose a random active-ish lag s with weight proportional to (c_s - 0.9 max + margin). Or choose top few lags.
- Choose lag sign and position. If choose positive lag s (m=center-s? or right?), to reduce c at positive: transfer from first to second (h_i -> h_{i+s}) where h_i>h_{i+s}. For m<center (positive), source i=p-s? Wait positive c_m (s>0) term h_i(1-h_{i+s}). To reduce, decrease h_i or increase h_{i+s}. Pair move: source index a (first), target b=a+s. Transfer δ=min(h_a,1-h_b, random): h_a-=δ; h_b+=δ.
- For negative lag (m>center, r>0): c = ∑ h_{i+r}(1-h_i). To reduce, decrease first (i+r) or increase second i. Transfer from source to target: source a (higher index i+r) to target b=a-r (lower). Transfer from a to b.
- Also if source<target? We'll ensure δ positive.
- To maintain sum, pair transfer preserves sum and bounds.
- We can try many random transfers, recompute c every e.g. 20 transfers, accept batch if cval lower, else revert. This is like batch coordinate.
- Since pair moves preserve sum exactly, no projection. Can run many cheap updates if we update c incrementally for two coordinates? Pair affects lags involving either index: union of affected lags O(n). We could update c incrementally for two coords O(n) instead of full correlate O(n^2). But full correlate in C maybe 0.5ms; 10k moves=5s. We can do recompute every batch.

```
import numpy as np
import time
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
except Exception:
    minimize = None

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(4):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            mask = h < 1.0
            cap = (1.0 - h) * mask
            tot = float(cap.sum())
            if tot <= 0:
                break
            h[mask] += (diff / tot) * cap[mask]
        else:
            mask = h > 0.0
            tot = float(h[mask].sum())
            if tot <= 0:
                break
            h[mask] += (diff / tot) * h[mask]
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    guard = 0
    while abs(diff) > 1e-11 and guard < n:
        if diff > 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            add = min(1.0 - h[idx], diff)
            h[idx] += add
            diff -= add
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            sub = min(h[idx], -diff)
            h[idx] -= sub
            diff += sub
        guard += 1
    return h
```

```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0,1)
    tgt=0.5*n
    for _ in range(3):
       d=tgt-h.sum()
       if abs(d)<1e-13: break
       if d>0:
          m=h<1; t=(1-h)[m].sum() or 1
          h[m]+=d*t? careful: h[m]+=(d/t)*(1-h[m])
       else:
          m=h>0; t=h[m].sum() or 1
          h[m]+=d/t*h[m]
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    d=tgt-h.sum()
    if d>1e-12:
       i=np.argmax(1-h); h[i]=min(1,h[i]+d)
    elif d<-1e-12:
       i=np.argmax(h); h[i]=max(0,h[i]+d)
    return h
```

`resample_h`:
```
def resample_h(h,n):
    if len(h)==n: return project_feasible(h,n)
    x0=(np.arange(len(h))+0.5)*(2.0/len(h)); x1=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(x1,x0,h),n)
```

`level_h` 10:
```
def level_h(n,rng):
    vals=np.linspace(0,1,5)
    out=np.empty(n)
    i=0
    while i<n:
       if n-i>=5:
          out[i:i+5]=vals[rng.permutation(5)]; i+=5
       else:
          out[i:]=vals[rng.choice(5,replacement=True,size=n-i)]; i=n
    return project_feasible(out,n)
```
Good.

```
def make_start(best,n,rng,typ):
    base=resample_h(best,n); x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2/n); typ%=7
    if typ==0:
       d=0
       for k in range(1,8): d += rng.normal(0,0.06/k)*np.cos(k*np.pi*(x-1)) + rng.normal(0,0.03/k)*np.sin(k*np.pi*(x-1))
       return project_feasible(base+d,n)
    if typ==1: return binary_top_h(base,n)
    if typ==2:
       out=np.zeros(n); out[rng.choice(n,n//2,replace=False)]=1; return out
    if typ==3: return project_feasible(0.5*(base+base[::-1]),n)
    if typ==4:
       h=base.copy(); l=rng.randint(0,n); w=max(5,rng.randint(5,n//5+1)); hi=min(n,l+w)
       hw=0.5+0.5*np.cos(np.pi*np.arange(hi-l)/max(1,w-1)); h[l:hi]+=rng.normal(0,0.08)*hw
       return project_feasible(h,n)
    if typ==5: return level_h(n,rng)
    return project_feasible(0.5*(base+(1-base[::-1])),n)
```

Let's attempt to write `optimize_coordinate` efficiently ~55 lines:

```
def coord_evals(xs,cseg,a,qconst,unaff):
    xs=np.asarray(xs,float)
    v=np.max(cseg[None,:]+xs[:,None]*a[None,:],axis=1) if a.size else 0*xs
    return np.maximum(np.maximum(v,qconst+xs*(1-xs)),unaff)

def optimize_coordinate(hp,cseg,a,qconst,unaff,cur,iters=16):
    m=a.size; cm=float(np.max(cseg)) if m else 0.0
    fcur=max(unaff,cm,qconst+hp*(1-hp)); fcur=max(fcur,cur)
    b=cseg-a*hp
    pos=a>1e-14; neg=a<-1e-14; zero=~(pos|neg)
    zmax=float(b[zero].max()) if m and zero.any() else -1e100
    low=max(0.0,unaff,qconst,zmax)
    if low>=fcur-1e-11: return hp,fcur
    high=fcur+1e-10
    a_p=a[pos]; a_n=a[neg]; b_p=b[pos]; b_n=b[neg]; pc=a_p.size; nc=a_n.size
    tol=1e-11
    for _ in range(iters):
       mid=(low+high)*0.5
       feas=mid>=unaff-tol and mid>=qconst-tol and mid>=zmax-tol
       if feas:
          L=0.0; U=1.0
          if nc:
             L=max(L,float(np.max((mid-b_n)/a_n)))
          if pc:
             U=min(U,float(np.min((mid-b_p)/a_p)))
          if L<=1+tol and U>=-tol:
             g=mid-qconst
             if g>0.25: feas=True
             elif g>=-tol:
                r1=0.5-0.5*np.sqrt(max(0.0,1-4*g)); r2=0.5+0.5*np.sqrt(max(0.0,1-4*g))
                feas = max(0,L)<=min(r1,U)+tol or max(r2,L)<=min(1.0,U)+tol
       high=mid if feas else low; low=mid if not feas else low
    t=high
    cand=[0.0,1.0,hp,0.5]
    if t>=unaff-tol and t>=qconst-tol and t>=zmax-tol:
       L=0.0; U=1.0
       if nc: L=max(L,float(np.max((t-b_n)/a_n)))
       if pc: U=min(U,float(np.min((t-b_p)/a_p)))
       if L<=1+tol and U>=-tol:
          g=t-qconst
          br=[]
          if g>0.25: br=[(0.0,1.0)]
          elif g>=-tol:
             r1=0.5-0.5*np.sqrt(max(0,1-4*g)); r2=0.5+0.5*np.sqrt(max(0,1-4*g)); br=[(0.0,r1),(r2,1.0)]
          for lq,uq in br:
             lb=max(0,L,lq); ub=min(1.0,U,uq)
             if lb<=ub+tol:
                midv=(lb+ub)*0.5; span=ub-lb
                cand += [lb,ub,midv,lb+0.25*span,lb+0.75*span]
    xs=np.clip(np.asarray(cand,float),0,1)
    vals=coord_evals(xs,cseg,a,qconst,unaff); ii=int(np.argmin(vals)); bf=float(vals[ii])
    if bf<fcur-1e-10: return float(xs[ii]),bf
    return hp,fcur
```
This is ~60.

Potential bug: if `m=0`, `pos` empty; `b` empty; `zero` empty; `zmax` set -1e100 via condition `if m and zero.any()`. Good. `pc/nc` 0. low works.

`cd_polish` compact:
```
def cd_polish(h,n,passes,deadline,project_every=10,max_noimprove=4,rng=None):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'); cur=float(c.max()); dx=2.0/n
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cur*dx; no=0
    center=n-1
    for it in range(passes):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       order=rng.permutation(n) if rng is not None else np.arange(n)
       for idx in order:
          p=int(idx)
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          hp=float(h[p])
          lo=min(p,center-p); hi=max(center+p,2*center-p); seg=hi-lo+1
          s=center-np.arange(lo,hi+1,dtype=int)
          j=p+s; i=p-s
          a=np.zeros(seg,float)
          vj=(j>=0)&(j<n); vi=(i>=0)&(i<n)
          if np.any(vj): a[vj]+=1.0-h[j[vj]]
          if np.any(vi): a[vi]-=h[i[vi]]
          off=center-lo
          cseg=np.empty(seg-1,float); asg=np.empty(seg-1,float)
          if off: cseg[:off]=c[lo:center]; asg[:off]=a[:off]
          if hi>center: cseg[off:]=c[center+1:hi+1]; asg[off:]=a[off+1:]
          qconst=float(c[center]-hp*(1-hp))
          unaff=0.0
          if lo: unaff=max(unaff,float(c[:lo].max()))
          if hi+1<len(c): unaff=max(unaff,float(c[hi+1:].max()))
          bx,bf=optimize_coordinate(hp,cseg,asg,qconst,unaff,cur,16)
          if bf<cur-1e-10:
             dv=bx-hp
             if off: c[lo:center]+=dv*asg[:off]
             if hi>center: c[center+1:hi+1]+=dv*asg[off:]
             c[center]=qconst+bx*(1-bx)
             h[p]=bx; cur=bf
          if (p+1)%project_every==0:
             h=project_feasible(h,n); c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full'); cur=float(c.max())
       h=project_feasible(h,n); c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full'); cur=float(c.max())
       cv=cur*dx
       if cv<best_c-1e-12:
          best_c=cv; best_h=h.copy(); no=0
       else:
          no+=1
          if no>=max_noimprove: break
    return best_h,best_c
```
~50 lines. Good.

`binary_hill`:
```
def binary_hill(h,n,steps,deadline,rng):
    h=binary_top_h(h,n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full'); cur=float(c.max()); dx=2.0/n
    best=h.copy(); best_c=cur*dx
    for _ in range(steps):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n-1))
       if j>=i: j+=1
       if h[i]==h[j]: continue
       oi=float(h[i]); oj=float(h[j]); h[i]=oj; h[j]=oi
       c2=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full'); nv=float(c2.max())
       if nv<cur-1e-13:
          cur=nv; best=h.copy(); best_c=cur*dx
       else:
          h[i]=oi; h[j]=oj
    return best,best_c
```
~20. Note if h values fractional? We call with binary_top initial so binary. If `h[i]==h[j]` with floats exact 0/1. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), 0.0, 1.0)
    tgt = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(4):
        d = tgt - float(h.sum())
        if abs(d) < 1e-12: break
        if d > 0:
            m = h < 1.0
            t = float((1.0 - h)[m].sum())
            if t <= 0: break
            h[m] += (d / t) * (1.0 - h[m])
        else:
            m = h > 0.0
            t = float(h[m].sum())
            if t <= 0: break
            h[m] += (d / t) * h[m]
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    d = tgt - float(h.sum())
    g = 0
    while abs(d) > 1e-11 and g < n:
        if d > 0:
            i = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            add = min(float(1.0 - h[i]), float(d))
            h[i] += add; d -= add
        else:
            i = int(np.argmax(h))
            sub = min(float(h[i]), float(-d))
            h[i] -= sub; d += sub
        g += 1
    return h

def compute_c(h, n):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(c.max()) * (2.0 / n)

def resample_h(h, n):
    old = len(h)
    if old == n:
        return project_feasible(h, n)
    x0 = (np.arange(old) + 0.5) * (2.0 / old)
    x1 = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(x1, x0, h), n)

def triangle_h(n):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    return project_feasible(1.0 - np.abs(x - 1.0), n)

def binary_top_h(h, n):
    out = np.zeros(n)
    k = n // 2
    idx = np.argpartition(h, n - k)[n - k:] if k > 0 else []
    out[idx] = 1.0
    return out

def level_h(n, rng):
    vals = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 5)
    out = np.empty(n)
    i = 0
    while i < n:
        if n - i >= 5:
            out[i:i+5] = vals[rng.permutation(5)]
            i += 5
        else:
            out[i:] = vals[rng.choice(5, replacement=True, size=n-i)]
            i = n
    return project_feasible(out, n)

def make_start(best, n, rng, typ):
    base = resample_h(best, n)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    typ %= 7
    if typ == 0:
        d = 0.0
        for k in range(1, 8):
            d += rng.normal(0.0, 0.05 / k) * np.cos(k * np.pi * (x - 1.0))
            d += rng.normal(0.0, 0.025 / k) * np.sin(k * np.pi * (x - 1.0))
        return project_feasible(base + d, n)
    if typ == 1:
        return binary_top_h(base, n)
    if typ == 2:
        out = np.zeros(n)
        out[rng.choice(n, n // 2, replace=False)] = 1.0
        return out
    if typ == 3:
        return project_feasible(0.5 * (base + base[::-1]), n)
    if typ == 4:
        h = base.copy()
        l = int(rng.randint(0, n))
        w = max(5, int(rng.randint(5, max(6, n // 5))))
        hi = min(n, l + w)
        hw = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * np.arange(hi - l) / max(1, w - 1))
        h[l:hi] += rng.normal(0.0, 0.08) * hw
        return project_feasible(h, n)
    if typ == 5:
        return level_h(n, rng)
    return project_feasible(0.5 * (base + (1.0 - base[::-1])), n)

def coord_evals(xs, cseg, a, qconst, unaff):
    xs = np.asarray(xs, dtype=float)
    if a.size:
        v = np.max(cseg[None, :] + xs[:, None] * a[None, :], axis=1)
    else:
        v = np.zeros_like(xs)
    return np.maximum(np.maximum(v, qconst + xs * (1.0 - xs)), unaff)

def optimize_coordinate(hp, cseg, a, qconst, unaff, cur, iters=18):
    m = a.size
    cm = float(np.max(cseg)) if m else 0.0
    fcur = max(unaff, cm, qconst + hp * (1.0 - hp))
    fcur = max(fcur, cur)
    b = cseg - a * hp
    pos = a > 1e-14
    neg = a < -1e-14
    zero = ~(pos | neg)
    zmax = float(b[zero].max()) if (m and zero.any()) else -1e100
    low = max(0.0, unaff, qconst, zmax)
    if low >= fcur - 1e-11:
        return hp, fcur
    high = fcur + 1e-10
    ap = a[pos]; an = a[neg]; bp = b[pos]; bn = b[neg]
    pc = ap.size; nc = an.size
    tol = 1e-11
    for _ in range(iters):
        mid = 0.5 * (low + high)
        feas = (mid >= unaff - tol) and (mid >= qconst - tol) and (mid >= zmax - tol)
        if feas:
            L = 0.0; U = 1.0
            if nc:
                L = max(L, float(np.max((mid - bn) / an)))
            if pc:
                U = min(U, float(np.min((mid - bp) / ap)))
            if L <= 1.0 + tol and U >= -tol:
                gg = mid - qconst
                if gg > 0.25:
                    feas = True
                elif gg >= -tol:
                    disc = max(0.0, 1.0 - 4.0 * gg)
                    r1 = 0.5 - 0.5 * np.sqrt(disc)
                    r2 = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.sqrt(disc)
                    feas = (max(0.0, L) <= min(r1, U) + tol) or (max(r2, L) <= min(1.0, U) + tol)
        if feas:
            high = mid
        else:
            low = mid
    t = high
    cand = [0.0, 1.0, hp, 0.5]
    if (t >= unaff - tol) and (t >= qconst - tol) and (t >= zmax - tol):
        L = 0.0; U = 1.0
        if nc:
            L = max(L, float(np.max((t - bn) / an)))
        if pc:
            U = min(U, float(np.min((t - bp) / ap)))
        if L <= 1.0 + tol and U >= -tol:
            gg = t - qconst
            branches = []
            if gg > 0.25:
                branches = [(0.0, 1.0)]
            elif gg >= -tol:
                disc = max(0.0, 1.0 - 4.0 * gg)
                r1 = 0.5 - 0.5 * np.sqrt(disc)
                r2 = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.sqrt(disc)
                branches = [(0.0, r1), (r2, 1.0)]
            for lq, uq in branches:
                lb = max(0.0, L, lq)
                ub = min(1.0, U, uq)
                if lb <= ub + tol:
                    midv = 0.5 * (lb + ub)
                    sp = ub - lb
                    cand.extend([lb, ub, midv, lb + 0.25 * sp, lb + 0.75 * sp])
    xs = np.clip(np.asarray(cand, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    vals = coord_evals(xs, cseg, a, qconst, unaff)
    ii = int(np.argmin(vals))
    bf = float(vals[ii])
    if bf < fcur - 1e-10:
        return float(xs[ii]), bf
    return hp, fcur

def cd_polish(h, n, passes, deadline, project_every=10, max_noimprove=4, rng=None):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    cur = float(c.max()); dx = 2.0 / n
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c = cur * dx
    no = 0; center = n - 1
    for _ in range(passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        order = rng.permutation(n) if rng is not None else np.arange(n)
        cnt = 0
        for idx in order:
            p = int(idx); cnt += 1
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            hp = float(h[p])
            lo = min(p, center - p)
            hi = max(center + p, 2 * center - p)
            seg = hi - lo + 1
            s = center - np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=int)
            j = p + s; i = p - s
            a = np.zeros(seg, dtype=float)
            vj = (j >= 0) & (j < n)
            vi = (i >= 0) & (i < n)
            if np.any(vj):
                a[vj] += 1.0 - h[j[vj]]
            if np.any(vi):
                a[vi] -= h[i[vi]]
            off = center - lo
            cseg = np.empty(seg - 1, dtype=float)
            asg = np.empty(seg - 1, dtype=float)
            if off:
                cseg[:off] = c[lo:center]
                asg[:off] = a[:off]
            if hi > center:
                cseg[off:] = c[center + 1:hi + 1]
                asg[off:] = a[off + 1:]
            qconst = float(c[center] - hp * (1.0 - hp))
            unaff = 0.0
            if lo:
                unaff = max(unaff, float(c[:lo].max()))
            if hi + 1 < c.size:
                unaff = max(unaff, float(c[hi + 1:].max()))
            bx, bf = optimize_coordinate(hp, cseg, asg, qconst, unaff, cur, 18)
            if bf < cur - 1e-10:
                dv = bx - hp
                if off:
                    c[lo:center] += dv * asg[:off]
                if hi > center:
                    c[center + 1:hi + 1] += dv * asg[off:]
                c[center] = qconst + bx * (1.0 - bx)
                h[p] = bx
                cur = bf
            if cnt % project_every == 0:
                h = project_feasible(h, n)
                c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
                cur = float(c.max())
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
        cur = float(c.max())
        cv = cur * dx
        if cv < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = cv
            best_h = h.copy()
            no = 0
        else:
            no += 1
            if no >= max_noimprove:
                break
    return best_h, best_c

def binary_hill(h, n, steps, deadline, rng):
    h = binary_top_h(h, n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    cur = float(c.max()); dx = 2.0 / n
    best = h.copy(); best_c = cur * dx
    for _ in range(steps):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        j = int(rng.randint(n - 1))
        if j >= i:
            j += 1
        if h[i] == h[j]:
            continue
        oi = float(h[i]); oj = float(h[j])
        h[i] = oj; h[j] = oi
        nc = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
        nv = float(nc.max())
        if nv < cur - 1e-13:
            cur = nv; best = h.copy(); best_c = cur * dx
        else:
            h[i] = oi; h[j] = oj
    return best, best_c

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    hard = t0 + float(budget_s) - 2.0
    deadline = hard - 30.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    has = False
    try:
        h = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        if h.size:
            has = True
    except Exception:
        h = triangle_h(320)
    n = len(h)
    if n % 2:
        n += 1
    if n < 96 or n > 600:
        n2 = int(min(600, max(96, n)))
        h = resample_h(h, n2); n = n2
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    if not has:
        best_h = h; best_c = compute_c(h, n)
        for typ in [2, 5, 0, 3, 1, 4, 6]:
            h0 = make_start(best_h, n, rng, typ)
            if typ == 2 and n <= 256:
                h0, _ = binary_hill(h0, n, 6000, deadline - 250.0, rng)
            h0, c0 = cd_polish(h0, n, 8, deadline - 300.0, 12, 2, rng)
            if c0 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h0, c0
        h = best_h; n = len(h)
    if time.time() < deadline - 120.0:
        h, c = cd_polish(h, n, 1000, deadline - 320.0, 10, 5, rng)
    else:
        c = compute_c(h, n)
    best_h = h; best_c = c; n_best = n
    target = 0.38075
    for _ in range(5000):
        if time.time() > deadline - 60.0 or best_c <= target:
            break
        r = rng.random_sample()
        if r < 0.45:
            nt = n_best
        elif r < 0.60:
            nt = 500
        elif r < 0.75:
            nt = 256
        elif r < 0.90:
            nt = 384
        else:
            nt = int(rng.choice([128, 160, 192, 224, 288, 360, 440, 560, 600]))
        if nt % 2:
            nt += 1
        typ = int(rng.randint(0, 7))
        h0 = make_start(best_h, nt, rng, typ)
        if nt <= 160:
            passes, pe, ms = 12, 12, 35.0
        elif nt <= 260:
            passes, pe, ms = 9, 12, 25.0
        elif nt <= 420:
            passes, pe, ms = 6, 12, 18.0
        else:
            passes, pe, ms = 5, 14, 14.0
        end_total = min(deadline - 60.0, time.time() + ms)
        if typ == 2 and nt <= 256 and end_total > time.time() + 6.0:
            h0, _ = binary_hill(h0, nt, 7000, time.time() + min(12.0, ms * 0.45), rng)
            end_total = max(time.time() + 3.0, min(end_total, time.time() + ms * 0.55))
        h1, c1 = cd_polish(h0, nt, passes, end_total, pe, 3, rng)
        if c1 < best_c - 1e-12:
            old = best_c
            best_h, best_c, n_best = h1, c1, nt
            if old - best_c > 1e-5 and time.time() < deadline - 120.0:
                h1, c1 = cd_polish(best_h, n_best, 300, deadline - 120.0, 10, 4, rng)
                if c1 < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c = h1, c1
    final_deadline = hard - 1.0
    best_h, best_c = cd_polish(best_h, n_best, 10000, final_deadline, 8, 3, rng)
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_c = compute_c(best_h, len(best_h))
    return best_h, best_c, len(best_h)
```

```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy(),0,1)
    tgt=0.5*n
    for _ in range(4):
       d=tgt-float(h.sum())
       if abs(d)<1e-12: break
       if d>0:
          m=h<1; t=float((1-h)[m].sum())
          if t>0: h[m]+=d/t*(1-h[m])
       else:
          m=h>0; t=float(h[m].sum())
          if t>0: h[m]+=d/t*h[m]
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    d=tgt-float(h.sum()); g=0
    while abs(d)>1e-11 and g<n:
       if d>0:
          i=int(np.argmax(1-h)); add=min(float(1-h[i]),float(d)); h[i]+=add; d-=add
       else:
          i=int(np.argmax(h)); sub=min(float(h[i]),float(-d)); h[i]-=sub; d+=sub
       g+=1
    return h
```

Let's design compact `smooth_refine` with L-BFGS-B:
```
from scipy.optimize import minimize
def lb_obj(x,n,alpha,pen):
    c=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,mode='full'); z=alpha*c; zm=float(z.max())
    v=(zm+float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z-zm))))/alpha)*(2.0/n)
    s=float(x.sum())-0.5*n
    return v+pen*s*s
def lb_jac(x,n,alpha,pen):
    c=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,mode='full'); z=alpha*c; z-=z.max(); w=np.exp(z); w/=w.sum()
    center=n-1; g=np.zeros(n)
    for m in range(2*n-1):
      wm=float(w[m]);
      if wm<=1e-15: continue
      s=center-m
      if s>0:
        g[:n-s]+=wm*(1.0-x[s:]); g[s:]-=wm*x[:n-s]
      elif s<0:
        r=-s; g[r:]+=wm*(1.0-x[:n-r]); g[:n-r]-=wm*x[r:]
      else: g+=wm*(1.0-2.0*x)
    s=float(x.sum())-0.5*n
    return (2.0/n)*g+2.0*pen*s
def smooth_refine(h,n,deadline,pen=80.0,alphas=(50,500),maxiter=25):
    if minimize is None: return h,compute_c(h,n)
    h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=compute_c(h,n); best=h; bounds=[(0,1)]*n
    for al in alphas:
       if time.time()>deadline-25: break
       try:
         res=minimize(lb_obj,best,args=(n,al,pen),method='L-BFGS-B',jac=lb_jac,bounds=bounds,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
         x=project_feasible(res.x,n); c=compute_c(x,n)
         if c<best_c: best,best_c=x,c
       except Exception: pass
    return best,best_c
```
~35 lines.

```
def binary_hill(h,n,steps,deadline,rng):
    h=binary_top_h(h,n); c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full'); cur=float(c.max()); dx=2/n; best=h.copy(); bc=cur*dx
    for _ in range(steps):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n-1)); j+= (j>=i)
       if h[i]==h[j]: continue
       oi,oj=h[i],h[j]; h[i]=oj; h[j]=oi
       nv=float(np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full').max())
       if nv<cur-1e-13: cur=nv; best=h.copy(); bc=cur*dx
       else: h[i]=oi; h[j]=oj
    return best,bc
```

```
import numpy as np, time
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
except Exception:
    minimize = None
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), 0, 1)
    tgt = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(4):
        d = tgt - float(h.sum())
        if abs(d) < 1e-12: break
        if d > 0:
            m = h < 1
            t = float((1 - h)[m].sum())
            if t > 0: h[m] += d / t * (1 - h[m])
        else:
            m = h > 0
            t = float(h[m].sum())
            if t > 0: h[m] += d / t * h[m]
        h = np.clip(h, 0, 1)
    d = tgt - float(h.sum()); g = 0
    while abs(d) > 1e-11 and g < n:
        if d > 0:
            i = int(np.argmax(1 - h)); ad = min(float(1 - h[i]), float(d)); h[i] += ad; d -= ad
        else:
            i = int(np.argmax(h)); ad = min(float(h[i]), float(-d)); h[i] -= ad; d += ad
        g += 1
    return h
...
```
Line count project 24? actually maybe 23.

`smooth_refine` compact:
```
def lb_obj(x,n,al,pen):
    c=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,mode='full'); z=al*c; zm=float(z.max())
    s=float(x.sum())-0.5*n
    return (zm+float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z-zm))))/al)*(2.0/n)+pen*s*s
def lb_jac(x,n,al,pen):
    c=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,mode='full'); z=al*c; z-=z.max(); w=np.exp(z); w/=w.sum()
    ce=n-1; g=np.zeros(n)
    for m in range(2*n-1):
        wm=float(w[m])
        if wm<=1e-15: continue
        s=ce-m
        if s>0:
            g[:n-s]+=wm*(1.0-x[s:]); g[s:]-=wm*x[:n-s]
        elif s<0:
            r=-s; g[r:]+=wm*(1.0-x[:n-r]); g[:n-r]-=wm*x[r:]
        else:
            g+=wm*(1.0-2.0*x)
    s=float(x.sum())-0.5*n
    return (2.0/n)*g+2.0*pen*s
def smooth_refine(h,n,deadline,pen=100.0,alphas=(50,500),maxiter=25):
    if minimize is None: return h, compute_c(h,n)
    h=project_feasible(h,n); best=h; bc=compute_c(h,n); bnd=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    for al in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 20: break
        try:
            res=minimize(lb_obj,best,args=(n,al,pen),method='L-BFGS-B',jac=lb_jac,bounds=bnd,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
            x=project_feasible(res.x,n); c=compute_c(x,n)
            if c<bc: best,bc=x,c
        except Exception: pass
    return best,bc
```

```python
import numpy as np, time
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
except Exception:
    minimize = None

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), 0, 1)
    tgt = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(4):
        d = tgt - float(h.sum())
        if abs(d) < 1e-12:
            break
        if d > 0:
            m = h < 1
            t = float((1 - h)[m].sum())
            if t > 0:
                h[m] += d / t * (1 - h[m])
        else:
            m = h > 0
            t = float(h[m].sum())
            if t > 0:
                h[m] += d / t * h[m]
        h = np.clip(h, 0, 1)
    d = tgt - float(h.sum())
    g = 0
    while abs(d) > 1e-11 and g < n:
        if d > 0:
            i = int(np.argmax(1 - h))
            ad = min(float(1 - h[i]), float(d))
            h[i] += ad
            d -= ad
        else:
            i = int(np.argmax(h))
            ad = min(float(h[i]), float(-d))
            h[i] -= ad
            d += ad
        g += 1
    return h

def compute_c(h, n):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(c.max()) * (2.0 / n)

def resample_h(h, n):
    old = len(h)
    if old == n:
        return project_feasible(h, n)
    x0 = (np.arange(old) + 0.5) * (2.0 / old)
    x1 = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(x1, x0, h), n)

def triangle_h(n):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    return project_feasible(1.0 - np.abs(x - 1.0), n)

def binary_top_h(h, n):
    k = n // 2
    out = np.zeros(n)
    if k:
        out[np.argpartition(h, n - k)[n - k:]] = 1.0
    return out

def level_h(n, rng):
    vals = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 5)
    out = np.empty(n)
    i = 0
    while i < n:
        if n - i >= 5:
            out[i:i + 5] = vals[rng.permutation(5)]
            i += 5
        else:
            out[i:] = vals[rng.choice(5, replacement=True, size=n - i)]
            i = n
    return project_feasible(out, n)

def make_start(best, n, rng, typ):
    base = resample_h(best, n)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    typ %= 5
    if typ == 0:
        d = 0.0
        for k in range(1, 8):
            d += rng.normal(0.0, 0.05 / k) * np.cos(k * np.pi * (x - 1.0))
            d += rng.normal(0.0, 0.025 / k) * np.sin(k * np.pi * (x - 1.0))
        return project_feasible(base + d, n)
    if typ == 1:
        return binary_top_h(base, n)
    if typ == 2:
        out = np.zeros(n)
        out[rng.choice(n, n // 2, replace=False)] = 1.0
        return out
    if typ == 3:
        if rng.random_sample() < 0.5:
            return project_feasible(0.5 * (base + base[::-1]), n)
        return project_feasible(0.5 * (base + (1.0 - base[::-1])), n)
    return level_h(n, rng)

def coord_evals(xs, cseg, a, qconst, unaff):
    xs = np.asarray(xs, dtype=float)
    if a.size:
        v = np.max(cseg[None, :] + xs[:, None] * a[None, :], axis=1)
    else:
        v = np.zeros_like(xs)
    return np.maximum(np.maximum(v, qconst + xs * (1.0 - xs)), unaff)

def optimize_coordinate(hp, cseg, a, qconst, unaff, cur, iters=18):
    m = a.size
    cm = float(np.max(cseg)) if m else 0.0
    fcur = max(unaff, cm, qconst + hp * (1.0 - hp))
    fcur = max(fcur, cur)
    b = cseg - a * hp
    pos = a > 1e-14
    neg = a < -1e-14
    zero = ~(pos | neg)
    zmax = float(b[zero].max()) if (m and zero.any()) else -1e100
    low = max(0.0, unaff, qconst, zmax)
    if low >= fcur - 1e-11:
        return hp, fcur
    high = fcur + 1e-10
    ap = a[pos]
    an = a[neg]
    bp = b[pos]
    bn = b[neg]
    pc = ap.size
    nc = an.size
    tol = 1e-11
    for _ in range(iters):
        mid = 0.5 * (low + high)
        feas = (mid >= unaff - tol) and (mid >= qconst - tol) and (mid >= zmax - tol)
        if feas:
            L = 0.0
            U = 1.0
            if nc:
                L = max(L, float(np.max((mid - bn) / an)))
            if pc:
                U = min(U, float(np.min((mid - bp) / ap)))
            if L <= 1.0 + tol and U >= -tol:
                gg = mid - qconst
                if gg > 0.25:
                    feas = True
                elif gg >= -tol:
                    disc = max(0.0, 1.0 - 4.0 * gg)
                    r1 = 0.5 - 0.5 * np.sqrt(disc)
                    r2 = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.sqrt(disc)
                    feas = (max(0.0, L) <= min(r1, U) + tol) or (max(r2, L) <= min(1.0, U) + tol)
        if feas:
            high = mid
        else:
            low = mid
    t = high
    cand = [0.0, 1.0, hp, 0.5]
    if (t >= unaff - tol) and (t >= qconst - tol) and (t >= zmax - tol):
        L = 0.0
        U = 1.0
        if nc:
            L = max(L, float(np.max((t - bn) / an)))
        if pc:
            U = min(U, float(np.min((t - bp) / ap)))
        if L <= 1.0 + tol and U >= -tol:
            gg = t - qconst
            br = []
            if gg > 0.25:
                br = [(0.0, 1.0)]
            elif gg >= -tol:
                disc = max(0.0, 1.0 - 4.0 * gg)
                r1 = 0.5 - 0.5 * np.sqrt(disc)
                r2 = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.sqrt(disc)
                br = [(0.0, r1), (r2, 1.0)]
            for lq, uq in br:
                lb = max(0.0, L, lq)
                ub = min(1.0, U, uq)
                if lb <= ub + tol:
                    cand.extend([lb, ub, 0.5 * (lb + ub)])
    xs = np.clip(np.asarray(cand, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    vals = coord_evals(xs, cseg, a, qconst, unaff)
    ii = int(np.argmin(vals))
    bf = float(vals[ii])
    if bf < fcur - 1e-10:
        return float(xs[ii]), bf
    return hp, fcur

def cd_polish(h, n, passes, deadline, project_every=10, max_noimprove=4, rng=None):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    cur = float(c.max())
    dx = 2.0 / n
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = cur * dx
    no = 0
    center = n - 1
    for _ in range(passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        order = rng.permutation(n) if rng is not None else np.arange(n)
        cnt = 0
        for idx in order:
            p = int(idx)
            cnt += 1
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            hp = float(h[p])
            lo = min(p, center - p)
            hi = max(center + p, 2 * center - p)
            seg = hi - lo + 1
            s = center - np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=int)
            j = p + s
            i = p - s
            a = np.zeros(seg, dtype=float)
            vj = (j >= 0) & (j < n)
            vi = (i >= 0) & (i < n)
            if np.any(vj):
                a[vj] += 1.0 - h[j[vj]]
            if np.any(vi):
                a[vi] -= h[i[vi]]
            off = center - lo
            cseg = np.empty(seg - 1, dtype=float)
            asg = np.empty(seg - 1, dtype=float)
            if off:
                cseg[:off] = c[lo:center]
                asg[:off] = a[:off]
            if hi > center:
                cseg[off:] = c[center + 1:hi + 1]
                asg[off:] = a[off + 1:]
            qconst = float(c[center] - hp * (1.0 - hp))
            unaff = 0.0
            if lo:
                unaff = max(unaff, float(c[:lo].max()))
            if hi + 1 < c.size:
                unaff = max(unaff, float(c[hi + 1:].max()))
            bx, bf = optimize_coordinate(hp, cseg, asg, qconst, unaff, cur, 18)
            if bf < cur - 1e-10:
                dv = bx - hp
                if off:
                    c[lo:center] += dv * asg[:off]
                if hi > center:
                    c[center + 1:hi + 1] += dv * asg[off:]
                c[center] = qconst + bx * (1.0 - bx)
                h[p] = bx
                cur = bf
            if cnt % project_every == 0:
                h = project_feasible(h, n)
                c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
                cur = float(c.max())
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
        cur = float(c.max())
        cv = cur * dx
        if cv < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = cv
            best_h = h.copy()
            no = 0
        else:
            no += 1
            if no >= max_noimprove:
                break
    return best_h, best_c

def binary_hill(h, n, steps, deadline, rng):
    h = binary_top_h(h, n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    cur = float(c.max())
    dx = 2.0 / n
    best = h.copy()
    bc = cur * dx
    for _ in range(steps):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        j = int(rng.randint(n - 1))
        if j >= i:
            j += 1
        if h[i] == h[j]:
            continue
        oi = float(h[i])
        oj = float(h[j])
        h[i] = oj
        h[j] = oi
        nv = float(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full').max())
        if nv < cur - 1e-13:
            cur = nv
            best = h.copy()
            bc = cur * dx
        else:
            h[i] = oi
            h[j] = oj
    return best, bc

def lb_obj(x, n, al, pen):
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode='full')
    z = al * c
    zm = float(z.max())
    s = float(x.sum()) - 0.5 * n
    return (zm + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z - zm)))) / al) * (2.0 / n) + pen * s * s

def lb_jac(x, n, al, pen):
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode='full')
    z = al * c
    z -= z.max()
    w = np.exp(z)
    w /= w.sum()
    ce = n - 1
    g = np.zeros(n)
    for m in range(2 * n - 1):
        wm = float(w[m])
        if wm <= 1e-15:
            continue
        s = ce - m
        if s > 0:
            g[:n - s] += wm * (1.0 - x[s:])
            g[s:] -= wm * x[:n - s]
        elif s < 0:
            r = -s
            g[r:] += wm * (1.0 - x[:n - r])
            g[:n - r] -= wm * x[r:]
        else:
            g += wm * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)
    s = float(x.sum()) - 0.5 * n
    return (2.0 / n) * g + 2.0 * pen * s

def smooth_refine(h, n, deadline, pen=100.0, alphas=(50, 500), maxiter=18):
    if minimize is None:
        return h, compute_c(h, n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best = h
    bc = compute_c(h, n)
    bnd = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    for al in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 20:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(lb_obj, best, args=(n, al, pen), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=lb_jac,
                           bounds=bnd, options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-12})
            x = project_feasible(res.x, n)
            c = compute_c(x, n)
            if c < bc:
                best, bc = x, c
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best, bc

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    hard = t0 + float(budget_s) - 2.0
    deadline = hard - 30.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    has = False
    try:
        h = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        has = bool(h.size)
    except Exception:
        h = None
    if not has:
        h = triangle_h(320)
    n = len(h)
    if n % 2:
        n += 1
    if n < 96 or n > 600:
        n = int(min(600, max(96, n)))
        h = resample_h(h, n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    if not has:
        best_h = h
        best_c = compute_c(h, n)
        for typ in [2, 4, 0, 3, 1]:
            h0 = make_start(best_h, n, rng, typ)
            if typ == 2 and n <= 256:
                h0, _ = binary_hill(h0, n, 6000, deadline - 250.0, rng)
            h0, c0 = cd_polish(h0, n, 8, deadline - 300.0, 12, 2, rng)
            if c0 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h0, c0
        h = best_h
        n = len(h)
    if time.time() < deadline - 120.0:
        h, c = cd_polish(h, n, 1000, deadline - 320.0, 10, 5, rng)
    else:
        c = compute_c(h, n)
    best_h = h
    best_c = c
    n_best = n
    if n_best <= 520 and time.time() < deadline - 160.0:
        h2, c2 = smooth_refine(best_h, n_best, deadline - 130.0, pen=80.0, alphas=(40, 400), maxiter=16)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h2, c2
        h2, c2 = cd_polish(best_h, n_best, 120, deadline - 100.0, 9, 4, rng)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h2, c2
    target = 0.38075
    for _ in range(5000):
        if time.time() > deadline - 60.0 or best_c <= target:
            break
        r = rng.random_sample()
        if r < 0.45:
            nt = n_best
        elif r < 0.60:
            nt = 500
        elif r < 0.75:
            nt = 256
        elif r < 0.90:
            nt = 384
        else:
            nt = int(rng.choice([128, 160, 192, 224, 288, 360, 440, 560, 600]))
        if nt % 2:
            nt += 1
        typ = int(rng.randint(0, 5))
        h0 = make_start(best_h, nt, rng, typ)
        if nt <= 160:
            passes, pe, ms = 12, 12, 35.0
        elif nt <= 260:
            passes, pe, ms = 9, 12, 25.0
        elif nt <= 420:
            passes, pe, ms = 6, 12, 18.0
        else:
            passes, pe, ms = 5, 14, 14.0
        end_total = min(deadline - 60.0, time.time() + ms)
        if typ == 2 and nt <= 256 and end_total > time.time() + 6.0:
            h0, _ = binary_hill(h0, nt, 7000, time.time() + min(12.0, ms * 0.45), rng)
            end_total = max(time.time() + 3.0, min(end_total, time.time() + ms * 0.55))
        h1, c1 = cd_polish(h0, nt, passes, end_total, pe, 3, rng)
        if c1 < best_c - 1e-12:
            old = best_c
            best_h, best_c, n_best = h1, c1, nt
            if old - best_c > 1e-5 and time.time() < deadline - 120.0:
                h1, c1 = cd_polish(best_h, n_best, 300, deadline - 120.0, 10, 4, rng)
                if c1 < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c = h1, c1
                if n_best <= 420 and time.time() < deadline - 110.0:
                    h2, c2 = smooth_refine(best_h, n_best, deadline - 100.0, pen=120.0, alphas=(80, 700), maxiter=18)
                    if c2 < best_c:
                        best_h, best_c = h2, c2
    final_deadline = hard - 1.0
    best_h, best_c = cd_polish(best_h, n_best, 10000, final_deadline, 8, 3, rng)
    if (minimize is not None) and (n_best <= 420) and (time.time() < final_deadline - 35.0):
        h2, c2 = smooth_refine(best_h, n_best, final_deadline - 20.0, pen=150.0, alphas=(200, 2000), maxiter=12)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h2, c2
        h2, c2 = cd_polish(best_h, n_best, 80, final_deadline - 3.0, 7, 2, rng)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h2, c2
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_c = compute_c(best_h, len(best_h))
    return best_h, best_c, len(best_h)
```
